We can't\n\t\t\t// allocate devices from it.\n\t\t\tif slice.Spec.Pool.Generation != generation {\n\t\t\t\tcomplete = false\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\t// We still need to keep incomplete pools, since we need to make sure\n\t\t// all devices available on a node is considered for allocationMode All.\n\t\tif !complete {\n\t\t\tincompletePools.Insert(poolID)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tpools := make(map[PoolID]*Pool)\n\tfor _, slice := range slices {\n\t\tif !features.PartitionableDevices && slice.Spec.PerDeviceNodeSelection != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif nodeName, allNodes := ptr.Deref(slice.Spec.NodeName, \"\"), ptr.Deref(slice.Spec.AllNodes, false); nodeName != \"\" || allNodes || slice.Spec.NodeSelector != nil {\n\t\t\tmatch, err := nodeMatches(node, nodeName, allNodes, slice.Spec.NodeSelector)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to perform node selection for slice %s: %w\", slice.Name, err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif match {\n\t\t\t\tif err := addSlice(pools, slice); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to add node slice %s: %w\", slice.Name, err)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else if ptr.Deref(slice.Spec.PerDeviceNodeSelection, false) {\n\t\t\tfor _, device := range slice.Spec.Devices {\n\t\t\t\tmatch, err := nodeMatches(node, ptr.Deref(device.NodeName, \"\"), ptr.Deref(device.AllNodes, false), device.NodeSelector)\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to perform node selection for device %s in slice %s: %w\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\tdevice.String(), slice.Name, err)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif match {\n\t\t\t\t\tif err := addSlice(pools, slice); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to add node slice %s: %w\", slice.Name, err)\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t// Nothing known was set. This must be some future, unknown extension,\n\t\t\t// so we don't know how to handle it. We may still be able to allocated from\n\t\t\t// other pools, so we continue.\n\t\t\t//\n\t\t\t// TODO (eventually): let caller decide how to report this to the user. Warning\n\t\t\t// about it for every slice on each scheduling attempt would be too noisy, but\n\t\t\t// perhaps once per run would be useful?\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n\t// Find incomplete pools and flatten into a single slice.\n\tresult := make([]*Pool, 0, len(pools))\n\tfor _, pool := range pools {\n\t\tpool.IsIncomplete = incompletePools.Has(poolIdentifier{driver: pool.Driver.String(), pool: pool.Pool.String()})\n\t\tpool.IsInvalid, pool.InvalidReason = poolIsInvalid(pool)\n\t\tresult = append(result, pool)\n\t}\n\n\treturn result, nil\n}\n\nfunc addSlice(pools map[PoolID]*Pool, s *resourceapi.ResourceSlice) error {\n\tvar slice draapi.ResourceSlice\n\tif err := draapi.Convert_v1_ResourceSlice_To_api_ResourceSlice(s, &slice, nil); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"convert ResourceSlice: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tid := PoolID{Driver: slice.Spec.Driver, Pool: slice.Spec.Pool.Name}\n\tpool := pools[id]\n\tif pool == nil {\n\t\t// New pool.\n\t\tpool = &Pool{\n\t\t\tPoolID: id,\n\t\t\tSlices: []*draapi.ResourceSlice{&slice},\n\t\t}\n\t\tpools[id] = pool\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tif slice.Spec.Pool.Generation < pool.Slices[0].Spec.Pool.Generation {","numLines":125,"startLine":45,"totalLines":212}}}

For each individual device we pick one possible\\n138: \\t// candidate after the other, checking constraints as we go.\\n139: \\t// Each chosen candidate is marked as \\\"in use\\\" and the process\\n140: \\t// continues, recursively. This way, all requests get matched against\\n141: \\t// all candidates in all possible orders.\\n142: \\t//\\n143: \\t// The first full solution is chosen.\\n144: \\t//\\n145: \\t// In other words, this is an exhaustive search. This is okay because\\n146: \\t// it aborts early. Once scoring gets added, more intelligence may be\\n147: \\t// needed to avoid trying \\\"equivalent\\\" solutions (two identical\\n148: \\t// requests, two identical devices, two solutions that are the same in\\n149: \\t// practice).\\n150: \\n151: \\t// This is where we sanity check that we can actually handle the claims\\n152: \\t// and their requests. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_012BR6QUC61nQmU59Q9uVm6V","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XmTqwj3vRrKrbah5TVsTUF","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/investigation.md","content":"# Investigation Report\n\n## Summary\n\nThe change to allow `AllocationMode: All` from multi-node resource pools (commit 2e534d6) modifies the `GatherPools` function in the DRA structured allocator to pre-compute pool completeness across **all** slices (including those not targeting the current node) before filtering. This affects the scheduler's Filter extension point hot path, where `Allocate()` is called for every candidate node. The change was already reverted (commit a0e500a) due to a confirmed performance regression, indicating that the current approach of iterating all slices upfront in `GatherPools` is too expensive for the scheduler's per-node evaluation loop.\n\n## Root Cause\n\nThe change modifies `GatherPools` in three allocator variants (stable, incubating, experimental) to:\n\n1. **Pre-index all slices by pool** (`slicesByPool` map) before any node filtering, adding an O(S) pass over all slices where S = total slices in the cluster.\n2. **Pre-compute incomplete pools** by checking slice counts and generations across all slices per pool, adding another O(S) pass.\n3. **Change the `IsIncomplete` check** from a local per-pool check (`len(pool.Slices) != ResourceSliceCount`) to a lookup into the pre-computed `incompletePools` set.\n\nThe core problem: previously, `GatherPools` would only process slices matching the target node. The change forces it to process **all** slices to determine global pool completeness, which is necessary for correctness (a multi-node pool with `AllocationMode: All` needs to know if the pool is complete globally, not just for one node's slices). However, this work scales with the total number of ResourceSlices in the cluster, not just those relevant to a node, and it runs on **every** `Filter` call — i.e., for every (pod, node) pair during scheduling.\n\n## Evidence\n\n### Core Change: Pool Completeness Check (3 files, identical change)\n\n**File: `staging/src/k8s.io/dynamic-resource-allocation/structured/internal/stable/pools_stable.go:48-95`** (local)\n- New `poolIdentifier` struct at line 48\n- `slicesByPool` pre-indexing at lines 59-65\n- `incompletePools` pre-computation at lines 71-95\n- `IsIncomplete` now uses `incompletePools.Has()` at line 143 instead of `int64(len(pool.Slices)) != pool.Slices[0].Spec.Pool.ResourceSliceCount`\n\n**Same change replicated in:**\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/dynamic-resource-allocation/structured/internal/incubating/pools_incubating.go`\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/dynamic-resource-allocation/structured/internal/experimental/pools_experimental.go`\n\n### Scheduler Hot Path — Where GatherPools Is Called\n\n**File: `staging/src/k8s.io/dynamic-resource-allocation/structured/internal/stable/allocator_stable.go:110-134`**\n- `Allocate()` calls `GatherPools()` at line 129 for every node evaluation\n- The `Allocator` is created once in `PreFilter` (dynamicresources.go:566) but `Allocate()` is called per-node in `Filter` (dynamicresources.go:715)\n\n**File: `pkg/scheduler/framework/plugins/dynamicresources/dynamicresources.go:631-744`**\n- `Filter()` at line 631 is the scheduler extension point\n- Calls `state.allocator.Allocate(allocCtx, node, claimsToAllocate)` at line 715\n- This runs for **every** node in the cluster during the filter phase\n\n### AllocationMode: All Logic in Allocator\n\n**File: `staging/src/k8s.io/dynamic-resource-allocation/structured/internal/stable/allocator_stable.go:446-481`**\n- When `AllocationMode` is `All`, the allocator iterates `pool.DeviceSlicesTargetingNode` (line 463) to pre-collect all matching devices\n- At line 456, it checks `pool.IsIncomplete` and returns an error if true — this is where multi-node pool completeness matters\n- At lines 860-914, the actual allocation loop for \"All\" mode uses the pre-collected `allDevices` list\n\n### API Type Definitions\n\n**File: `pkg/apis/resource/types.go:1086-1092`**\n```go\ntype DeviceAllocationMode string\nconst (\n    DeviceAllocationModeExactCount = DeviceAllocationMode(\"ExactCount\")\n    DeviceAllocationModeAll        = DeviceAllocationMode(\"All\")\n)\n```\n\nIdentical definitions in:\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/api/resource/v1/types.go:1143-1150`\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/api/resource/v1beta1/types.go:1160-1167`\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/api/resource/v1beta2/types.go:1149-1156`\n\n### Validation\n\n**File: `pkg/apis/resource/validation/validation.go:268-286`**\n- `validateDeviceAllocationMode()` validates that `AllocationMode: All` does not specify a count\n- Called for both `ExactDeviceRequest` (line 261) and `DeviceSubRequest` (line 250)\n\n### Quota Evaluator\n\n**File: `pkg/quota/v1/evaluator/core/resource_claims.go:249-277`**\n- For `DeviceAllocationModeAll`, assumes worst-case `AllocationResultsMaxSize` devices for quota purposes (lines 250-251, 275-277)\n- This is unchanged by the commit, but becomes more impactful if multi-node pools expose more devices per claim\n\n### Kubelet DRA Manager\n\n**File: `pkg/kubelet/cm/dra/manager.go:237-320`**\n- `prepareResources()` processes the allocation result received from the scheduler\n- It calls `NodePrepareResources` on the DRA plugin for each claim\n- The kubelet is allocation-mode agnostic — it only processes the final `AllocationResult`\n\n### Node Selector Construction\n\n**File: `staging/src/k8s.io/dynamic-resource-allocation/structured/internal/stable/allocator_stable.go:1377-1420`**\n- `createNodeSelector()` at line 1379 constructs the node affinity for the allocation\n- When `NodeName` is set on a device/slice (line 1397), the allocation is pinned to that specific node\n- This is the mechanism that constrains `AllocationMode: All` results to node-local devices\n\n### Test Coverage\n\n**File: `staging/src/k8s.io/dynamic-resource-allocation/structured/internal/allocatortesting/allocator_testing.go`**\n- `\"allocation-mode-all-with-multi-host-resource-pool\"` test at rejected for All mode\n- `\"all-devices-plus-another\"` at line 1279: All mode combined with ExactCount\n\n**Other test files referencing AllocationMode:**\n- `pkg/apis/resource/validation/validation_resourceclaim_test.go:462-481` — validation tests\n- `pkg/registry/resource/resourceclaim/strategy_test.go:50-106`\n- `pkg/registry/resource/resourceclaim/declarative_validation_test.go:516-550`\n- `pkg/apis/resource/fuzzer/fuzzer.go:39-52`\n\n### Performance Test Configuration\n\n**File: `test/integration/scheduler_perf/dra/performance-config.yaml`**\n- `SchedulingWithResourceClaimTemplate` benchmarks with 5000 nodes × 10 claims/node = 50,000 slices\n- The change adds O(50,000) additional work per `Filter` call in the `GatherPools` pre-scan\n- For 500 nodes with 2500 measure pods, this means ~500 × 2500 = 1.25M additional slice iterations\n\n### Commit History\n\n- **2e534d6** (2025-10-08, Morten Torkildsen): Original change \"DRA: Allow AllocationMode: All from multi-node resource pools\"\n- **83504f1** (2025-10-31): Merge of PR #134466\n- **a0e500a** (2025-11-03, Patrick Ohly): Revert — \"This reverts commit 2e534d6da100. It caused a performance regression, a different fix is needed.\"\n\n## Affected Components\n\n### HIGH RISK — Performance-Critical (Scheduler Hot Path)\n\n| Package | File | Impact |\n|---------|------|--------|\n| `structured/internal/stable` | `pools_stable.go:48-148` | GatherPools pre-scan of all slices runs per-node in Filter; O(S) per Filter call |\n| `structured/internal/incubating` | `pools_incubating.go` | Same GatherPools change |\n| `structured/internal/experimental` | `pools_experimental.go` | Same GatherPools change |\n| `structured/internal/stable` | `allocator_stable.go:110-134,446-481` | Allocate() entry point + AllocationMode handling |\n| `scheduler/framework/plugins/dynamicresources` | `dynamicresources.go:631-744` | Filter() calls Allocate() per-node |\n\n### MEDIUM RISK — Correctness-Sensitive (Allocation Logic)\n\n| Package | File | Impact |\n|---------|------|--------|\n| `structured/internal/stable` | `allocator_stable.go:856-914` | \"doAllDevices\" allocation loop — if pool boundary changes, wrong device set may be allocated |\n| `structured/internal/stable` | `allocator_stable.go:1377-1420` | createNodeSelector — multi-node pools may produce different node constraints |\n| `quota/v1/evaluator/core` | `resource_claims.go:249-277` | Quota evaluation assumes max devices for All mode; more devices per claim if pools span nodes |\n| `apis/resource/validation` | `validation.go:267-286` | No change needed, but validates AllocationMode is well-formed |\n\n### LOW RISK — Downstream Consumers (Allocation-Mode Agnostic)\n\n| Package | File | Impact |\n|---------|------|--------|\n| `kubelet/cm/dra` | `manager.go:237-320` | Processes allocation result; agnostic to AllocationMode — sees only final device list |\n| `kubelet/cm` | `container_manager_linux.go:134` | Holds reference to DRA manager |\n| `client-go/applyconfigurations/resource/v1` | `exactdevicerequest.go`, `devicesubrequest.go` | Generated apply config builders — no logic change |\n| `client-go/applyconfigurations/resource/v1beta1` | Multiple files | Same — generated code |\n| `client-go/applyconfigurations/resource/v1beta2` | Multiple files | Same — generated code |\n| `api/resource/v1` | `types.go`, `generated.proto` | Type definitions only |\n\n### NOT AFFECTED — No Behavioral Change\n\n| Package | File | Reason |\n|---------|------|--------|\n| `apis/resource/fuzzer` | `fuzzer.go` | Fuzz testing helper |\n| `api/resourceclaimspec` | `util_test.go` | Test data only |\n| E2E test driver | `test/e2e/dra/test-driver/app/kubeletplugin.go` | Test infrastructure |\n\n## Recommendation\n\n### Risk Assessment\n\nThe change was **already reverted** (a0e500a) due to a confirmed performance regression. The fundamental issue is an architectural mismatch: the correctness requirement (global pool completeness for `AllocationMode: All`) conflicts with the performance requirement (per-node Filter must be fast).\n\n**What could break if this change has bugs:**\n1. **Performance regression (confirmed):** The O(S) pre-scan in `GatherPools` runs per-node during Filter, scaling with total cluster slice count. In a 5000-node cluster with 10 slices/node, this adds 50,000 iterations per Filter call.\n2. **False incomplete pool detection:** If the `incompletePools` set incorrectly marks a pool as incomplete (e.g., due to stale generation data), `AllocationMode: All` claims would fail allocation even when devices are available on the target node.\n3. **Over-allocation from multi-node pools:** If `DeviceSlicesTargetingNode` is not correctly filtered for the target node, `AllocationMode: All` could attempt to allocate devices not available on the scheduled node, leading to kubelet NodePrepareResources failures.\n4. **Quota over-counting:** Multi-node pools exposing more devices per-node could cause quota evaluator to over-count, though the worst-case `AllocationResultsMaxSize` assumption already handles this conservatively.\n\n### Recommended Testing Plan\n\n1. **Performance benchmarks (mandatory before re-landing):**\n   - Run `SchedulingWithResourceClaimTemplate/5000pods_500nodes` from `test/integration/scheduler_perf/dra/performance-config.yaml`\n   - Compare scheduling throughput before/after the change\n   - Target: no more than 10% regression vs baseline (current threshold: 40 pods/sec)\n\n2. **Unit tests (already present, extend for edge cases):**\n   - The `\"allocation-mode-all-with-multi-host-resource-pool\"` test in `allocator_testing.go` covers the basic case\n   - Add tests for: incomplete multi-node pools, multi-node pools with mixed generations, multi-node pools with `PerDeviceNodeSelection`\n\n3. **Integration tests:**\n   - Test scheduling with `AllocationMode: All` claims where pools span 2+ nodes\n   - Verify that only node-local devices are allocated\n   - Verify that incomplete multi-node pools correctly block allocation\n\n4. **E2E tests:**\n   - The `multiNodeTests` function in `test/e2e/dra/dra.go:1150` provides the multi-node test framework\n   - Add a test case that creates a multi-node resource pool and verifies `AllocationMode: All` allocates correctly\n\n### Risk Mitigation Strategy\n\nThe correct fix should avoid the per-node O(S) pre-scan. Potential approaches:\n\n1. **Cache pool completeness in the `Allocator`** (created once in PreFilter, reused across Filter calls): compute the `incompletePools` set once during `NewAllocator()` rather than on every `Allocate()` → `GatherPools()` call.\n\n2. **Lazy evaluation:** Only check global pool completeness when `AllocationMode: All` is actually requested. Skip the pre-scan for `ExactCount` claims.\n\n3. **Pre-indexed pool metadata:** Maintain a shared, event-driven cache of pool completeness status in the `DefaultDRAManager`, updated incrementally as ResourceSlice informer events arrive.\n\nOption 1 is the simplest and most aligned with the existing architecture. The `Allocator` is already created once per scheduling cycle in `PreFilter` (dynamicresources.go:566) and reused for all `Filter` calls. Moving the pre-scan into the constructor amortizes the cost.\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":103,"cache_read_input_tokens":129047,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":103,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b2cd408a-d298-40c6-a65a-421f7544a628","uuid":"3bee537c-4672-48aa-89da-ef84b752ca8e"}

This affects the scheduler's Filter extension point hot path, where `Allocate()` is called for every candidate node. The change was already reverted (commit a0e500a) due to a confirmed performance regression, indicating that the current approach of iterating all slices upfront in `GatherPools` is too expensive for the scheduler's per-node evaluation loop.\n\n## Root Cause\n\nThe change modifies `GatherPools` in three allocator variants (stable, incubating, experimental) to:\n\n1. **Pre-index all slices by pool** (`slicesByPool` map) before any node filtering, adding an O(S) pass over all slices where S = total slices in the cluster.\n2. **Pre-compute incomplete pools** by checking slice counts and generations across all slices per pool, adding another O(S) pass.\n3. **Change the `IsIncomplete` check** from a local per-pool check (`len(pool.Slices) != ResourceSliceCount`) to a lookup into the pre-computed `incompletePools` set.\n\nThe core problem: previously, `GatherPools` would only process slices matching the target node. The change forces it to process **all** slices to determine global pool completeness, which is necessary for correctness (a multi-node pool with `AllocationMode: All` needs to know if the pool is complete globally, not just for one node's slices). However, this work scales with the total number of ResourceSlices in the cluster, not just those relevant to a node, and it runs on **every** `Filter` call — i.e., for every (pod, node) pair during scheduling.\n\n## Evidence\n\n### Core Change: Pool Completeness Check (3 files, identical change)\n\n**File: `staging/src/k8s.io/dynamic-resource-allocation/structured/internal/stable/pools_stable.go:48-95`** (local)\n- New `poolIdentifier` struct at line 48\n- `slicesByPool` pre-indexing at lines 59-65\n- `incompletePools` pre-computation at lines 71-95\n- `IsIncomplete` now uses `incompletePools.Has()` at line 143 instead of `int64(len(pool.Slices)) != pool.Slices[0].Spec.Pool.ResourceSliceCount`\n\n**Same change replicated in:**\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/dynamic-resource-allocation/structured/internal/incubating/pools_incubating.go`\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/dynamic-resource-allocation/structured/internal/experimental/pools_experimental.go`\n\n### Scheduler Hot Path — Where GatherPools Is Called\n\n**File: `staging/src/k8s.io/dynamic-resource-allocation/structured/internal/stable/allocator_stable.go:110-134`**\n- `Allocate()` calls `GatherPools()` at line 129 for every node evaluation\n- The `Allocator` is created once in `PreFilter` (dynamicresources.go:566) but `Allocate()` is called per-node in `Filter` (dynamicresources.go:715)\n\n**File: `pkg/scheduler/framework/plugins/dynamicresources/dynamicresources.go:631-744`**\n- `Filter()` at line 631 is the scheduler extension point\n- Calls `state.allocator.Allocate(allocCtx, node, claimsToAllocate)` at line 715\n- This runs for **every** node in the cluster during the filter phase\n\n### AllocationMode: All Logic in Allocator\n\n**File: `staging/src/k8s.io/dynamic-resource-allocation/structured/internal/stable/allocator_stable.go:446-481`**\n- When `AllocationMode` is `All`, the allocator iterates `pool.DeviceSlicesTargetingNode` (line 463) to pre-collect all matching devices\n- At line 456, it checks `pool.IsIncomplete` and returns an error if true — this is where multi-node pool completeness matters\n- At lines 860-914, the actual allocation loop for \"All\" mode uses the pre-collected `allDevices` list\n\n### API Type Definitions\n\n**File: `pkg/apis/resource/types.go:1086-1092`**\n```go\ntype DeviceAllocationMode
